利率上行引發波動，惟無損 AI 供應鏈擴張動能
2026 年 10 月
9 月全球股市普遍回落，主因為油價急升、全球長端殖利率攀高及主要央行轉鷹。僅有日本股市
受 AI、半導體需求與企業信心支撐，抵銷 BOJ 升息壓力，成為主要市場中唯一正報酬。標普 500 9
月小跌 0.3%，雖 AI 與大型科技股獲利韌性仍在，但 Fed 於 9 月升息並暗示年底前再度升息，加上
油價與美債殖利率走高，壓抑估值並引發科技股震盪。印度股市下跌 6.9%最弱，主要受油價飆升、
全球利率上行及外資單月淨流出約 27 億美元拖累。中國股市下跌 4.7%，因受內需修復偏弱、科技

股評價承壓所致；歐洲股市也因油價單月大漲近 14%與全球殖利率攀升影響，9 月下跌 4.6%。
9 月主要債市全面下跌，核心壓力來自全球殖利率急升。美歐日央行同步升息、Brent 原油單月
上漲約 14%、 美國經濟數據偏強與公債供給疑慮 ， 使美國10 年期殖利率單月上升約 53bp，創 2022
年以來最大升幅。主要券種中，全球投資級公司債下跌 2.93%表現最弱，新興美元債亦下跌 2.91%，
因 9 月全球公債殖利率快速攀升，此兩類債券對利率變化均較敏感 ； 其中投資級公司債相較高收益債
存續期較長、票息緩衝較低，因此在利率重新定價期間承受較大價格壓力。亞洲高收益債下挫 1.20%
相對抗跌，因高票息與較短存續期提供緩衝；新興當地債跌 1.74%，亦優於多數美元債種。
在股市方面，AI 基礎建設需求持續擴張，帶動 HBM、伺服器 DRAM、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供
應鏈維持成長動能，市場表現由全面上漲逐步轉向結構性分化。若AI 伺服器需求持續成長且 CSP 資
本支出維持高檔，具供給優勢、定價能力及較高訂單能見度之企業，其營運表現有機會獲得支撐。整

體而言 ， 科技產業與台灣及日本股市獲利表現仍具關注度 ， 惟後續仍須留意全球景氣 、 產業供需變化 、
政策監管及市場評價調整等風險因素。
就債市而言，雖然貨幣政策風險下降有助於穩定長期通膨預期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶
來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率
環境下，中天期債券於票息收益與存續期間風險之間相對較為均衡。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級
公司債指數利差約 80 個基點 ， 雖然利差偏窄 ， 但高基準利率環境仍提供相對較高的收益水準 。 因此，
在目前市場環境下 ， 中天期美國公債及投資級債仍為值得持續觀察的債券資產類別 ， 惟後續表現仍須
留意利率走勢、信用利差變化及經濟成長動能等因素。

美國股市：通膨與升息預期上升推動美債利率上漲，美股呈高檔區間震盪
9 月因美伊關係緊張升溫 ， 推升油價並加劇市場對通膨回升的擔憂 ；相較於 6 月點陣圖 ，9 月FOMC
官員全面上修 2026 至 2027 年的政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示 2026 年底前會再升息一次，加上美
國經濟數據表現優於預期，使美國公債殖利率走升，進而壓抑美股表現。在此背景下，9 月標普 500
指數呈現高檔區間震盪格局。產業表現方面，資訊科技與通訊服務類股相對強勢，持續扮演指數上漲
的主要動能；相較之下，醫療保健、必需消費及部分工業類股表現較為疲弱。

9 月美國聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 的表決結果 ， 將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調25 個基點至
3.75%到 4.00%。9 月最新經濟預測，美國聯準會官員上修 2026 年核心 PCE 至 3.4%，2027 年維持
在 2.5%，將 2026-2027 年經濟成長上調 0.1%至 2.3%與 2.4%，顯示聯準會認為美國經濟與通膨仍
具韌性。
觀察美國近期的經濟數據可以發現企業投資動能依然強健，元大投顧表示，美國8 月扣除飛機與
國防的核心資本財訂單由前期的月增 0.6%加速至 1.6%， 其中初級金屬 、 機械及電機設備訂單均呈現
增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成長形
成穩健貢獻。
自 6 月底以來 ， 市場持續上修標普500 指數企業獲利預估 ， 其中以科技與能源類股貢獻最為明顯 ，
反映企業營運表現優於市場預期。AI 投資循環持續推進、企業數位轉型需求增加，以及美國內需維
持穩健，共同支撐企業獲利成長動能，並有助於吸收高利率環境帶來的壓力。與此同時，標普 500

指數前瞻本益比已由先前高點回落，評價壓力較先前明顯緩解。隨著企業獲利持續改善，美股行情正
由過去仰賴資金與情緒推升評價 ， 逐步轉向由基本面支撐股價 ， 後續表現將更取決於企業獲利是否持
續優於預期。
投資策略方面，投資焦點已逐漸由評價擴張轉向企業獲利成長與基本面表現。短期市場仍須留意
政策面不確定性 ， 美中貿易談判進展將牽動全球供應鏈與科技產業展望 ， 若關稅或科技限制措施再度
升溫，可能對科技股評價造成壓力；此外，美國期中選舉逐漸接近，企業稅率、科技監管及產業政策
等議題亦可能影響市場風險偏好。整體而言，企業獲利改善仍是支撐美股最重要的力量，若 AI 投資
循環與獲利成長趨勢延續，美股中長期表現仍可望由基本面驅動。

歐洲股市：儘管企業獲利上修，仍需持續關注地緣政治發展
9 月泛歐 600 指數下跌約 2%，產業表現方面 ，製藥 、生技、銀行及部分科技類股表現相對強勢，
顯示資金偏好具獲利能見度與防禦特性的族群 ； 相較之下 ， 礦業 、 消費及部分金融服務類股表現落後 ，
反映市場對歐洲經濟成長及企業獲利前景仍持審慎態度。整體而言，受到高油價以及高利率環境，投
資氣氛轉趨保守，市場資金持續聚焦於基本面較穩健的產業。
歐洲央行 9 月政策利率由 2.25% 升息 1 碼至 2.50 % ，符合市場預期。歐洲央行最新經濟預
測中上修中期通膨展望，2027 年消費者物價指數年增率由 2.3% 升至 2.5% ，2028 年則由 2.0%

上調至 2.1% ；核心消費者物價指數方面，2027 年和 2028 年預測亦各上修 0.1 % 至 2.6% 及
2.3% ，能源成本上升對食品及其他價格的傳導，使得通膨回落速度較原先預期緩慢。ECB 總裁拉加
德表示能源價格上漲逐步傳導至核心及食品通膨，預期整體通膨在 2027 年上半年維持在高檔。
根據花旗歐洲獲利修正指數(Earnings Revision Index) ，歐洲企業獲利預估已連續上修並到近五
年高點，反映企業基本面持續改善。受惠於歐元區景氣穩步復甦、製造業景氣回升，以及企業資本支
出逐步回暖，工業、金融及景氣循環類股獲利展望同步改善。隨著市場對企業未來盈餘預期提高，有
助支撐歐股評價面與資金流入，並成為推動泛歐 600 指數表現的重要動能。
然而，自 3 月美伊衝突爆發以來，市場對能源供給中斷的擔憂持續升溫，帶動油價走高，進而增
加仰賴進口能源的歐洲企業成本壓力，並壓抑歐股相對表現。進入9 月後，俄羅斯與北約緊張關係再
度升溫，歐洲安全局勢不確定性提高，進一步加劇市場對能源供應與地緣政治風險的擔憂。若相關衝

突持續延燒，不僅可能推升能源價格與通膨壓力，也可能影響企業投資意願及市場風險偏好，進而對
歐洲企業獲利與股市表現形成壓力。
展望 10 月，歐股仍將受到高油價、地緣政治風險及歐洲央行維持偏鷹立場等因素影響，市場波
動性可能維持偏高。近期能源價格攀升推升通膨預期，使 ECB 傾向維持較高利率水準，不僅提高企
業融資成本，也限制股市評價進一步擴張的空間。此外，中東局勢持續緊張，以及俄羅斯與北約間的
緊張關係再度升高，均可能影響投資人風險偏好，增添歐洲金融市場不確定性。雖然歐元區經濟活動
仍維持溫和擴張，企業獲利預估亦持續改善，但在高利率與地緣政治風險尚未明顯緩解之前，市場情
緒仍偏向審慎，後續仍需持續關注能源價格、通膨走勢及地緣政治發展，作為判斷歐股能否重啟上漲
動能的重要觀察指標。

日本股市：升息落地，景氣展望佳，第四季半導體與商社企業獲利動能有望延續
9 月日本股市受升息預期及長天期公債殖利率走高影響，部分高評價股票出現震盪，但這波調整
較偏向政策轉折下的評價重估，而非企業基本面全面轉弱。日本銀行於 9 月 18 日決議將短期政策利
率目標提高至約 1.25%，使市場反覆討論的升息方向逐漸明朗。隨著政策落地，投資焦點有望由利率
變動轉回企業營運與獲利。從正面角度看，利率正常化反映日本經濟具備一定韌性，股價整理也有助
於消化先前累積的評價壓力。若後續企業財測保持穩健，9 月的震盪可望成為第四季行情重新蓄勢的

過程。
日本股市的中期支撐，來自薪資成長、內需改善與企業投資的相互帶動。2026 年春鬥最終統計
顯示，5,368 個工會的平均調薪幅度達 5.01%，連續第三年維持在 5%以上；可明確辨識的基本薪資
調升幅度亦達 3.50%。薪資持續增加，有助於提升家庭消費能力，為零售與服務等內需產業提供較穩
定的需求基礎。若企業能透過產品競爭力、定價能力及經營效率吸收成本，消費回溫便有機會轉化為
獲利成長。同時，全球 AI 相關投資也為日本半導體設備、載板及電子零組件供應鏈創造機會，使內
需復甦與科技需求形成雙重支撐。
展望 10 月，隨著升息議題逐步由預期轉為已知條件，市場可望更加重視第四季訂單、企業財測
及實際獲利。科技產業方面，若全球 AI 相關資本支出持續擴大，日本具技術優勢的設備與零組件企
業，有機會將需求轉化為接單及出貨成長；商社與資源相關企業，則可觀察能源、原物料業務及投資
收益對現金流的貢獻。內需產業亦值得留意薪資成長能否帶動消費，以及企業能否維持利潤率。整體
而言，10 月的投資機會不必侷限於單一題材，市場有望逐步從政策消息主導，轉向由產業需求與企

業業績支撐的行情。
投資策略方面，半導體供應鏈可優先挑選訂單能見度高、具技術門檻且獲利成長明確的企業；商
社則著重現金流、資本配置與股東回饋，內需股則觀察消費回溫能否反映在營收及利潤。若股價因利
率或匯率波動而短暫回落，但企業基本面與財測未明顯改變，可考慮分批布局，避免追逐短線漲幅。
後續仍須追蹤長天期利率 、 日圓及企業財測變化 ， 但投資主軸可更積極地放在具備實質成長能力的公
司。日本央行升息落地後，企業獲利能否接棒，將是日本股市延續表現的關鍵。

台灣股市：訂單持續暢旺突破千億，AI 需求持續向供應鏈擴散，廠商展望樂觀
9 月觀察台股基本面，最明確的支撐來自 8 月外銷訂單創下單月新高，以及企業對 9 月接單維持
擴張預期。經濟部公布，8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6 億美元，月增 5.1%、年增 71.4%；其中資訊通信
與電子產品合計 799.6 億美元，占整體 77.7%，顯示 AI 伺服器、半導體與雲端建設仍是主要成長來
源。受查廠商對 9 月接單的預期，以訂單金額計算的動向指數為 52.1，高於代表持平的 50，意味整
體接單有望較 8 月增加。雖然 9 月實際結果仍待公布，但已發布的數據與企業展望，為台股進入第

四季提供了正面的基本面依據。
更值得留意的是，AI 需求正從核心晶片延伸至更廣的供應鏈。雲端業者擴充運算能力，除了帶動
伺服器與半導體接單，也使自研 ASIC、IC 設計及相關電子零組件成為後續觀察重點；半導體投資與
設備擴產 ， 則為自動化設備 、 銅箔及高階材料創造機會 。8 月機械與基本金屬外銷訂單分別年增 28.3%
及 26.8%，顯示科技投資的效益已反映在部分非電子貨品。對台股而言，這有助於投資主線從少數大
型權值股，延伸至具技術優勢與訂單能見度的中上游企業。不過，面板需求仍偏弱，產業表現並非全
面同步，選股仍須辨別企業實際受惠程度。
展望 10 月，市場焦點可望轉向第三季財報、第四季營運展望，以及 9 月外銷訂單能否驗證接單
續強。就廠商對 9 月的預期而言，資訊通信與電子產品的訂單金額動向指數分別為 52.4 及 51.5，顯
示科技需求仍具支撐；基本金屬與化學品則達 59.0 及 61.0，呈現部分材料領域接單改善的期待。若
AI 伺服器出貨 、 自研晶片開發及半導體資本支出持續推進 ， 相關台灣供應鏈的營收與獲利有望受惠。

另一方面，機械與光學器材指數分別為 46.3 及 49.5，提醒市場不能將整體訂單成長視為所有產業同
步復甦，仍須以後續實績檢驗。
投資策略方面，10 月以「掌握 AI 主線、尋找擴散機會、重視獲利驗證」作為配置方向。核心部
位可留意伺服器、半導體及 IC 設計等需求較明確的企業，並觀察 ASIC 相關業務能否由開發逐步轉
化為穩定出貨；其他機會則可從設備、銅箔與高階材料中，篩選確實取得訂單的公司。選股不只看產
業接單增幅，更應比較個別企業的營收成長、毛利率與獲利展望，避免在評價已高時追逐短期題材。
整體而言，台股仍有基本面支撐，後續行情能否擴大，關鍵在於 AI 需求能否持續轉化為更多企業的
實際獲利。

中國股市：AI 產業規模擴張難掩獲利困境，產業進入變現考驗
9 月中國 AI 產業的討論，從模型能力與使用量，逐漸轉向更根本的問題：在高階晶片取得受限、
業者競爭激烈的環境下，龐大的投入能否形成可持續的回報。9 月中美高層會談後，雙方宣布建立
AI 相關對話機制，但公開成果未顯示高階 AI 晶片出口限制已放寬。政策溝通有所進展，並未立即改
變企業取得先進算力的條件 。 與此同時 ， 低價及開放權重策略持續降低AI 使用門檻 ， 帶動市場擴張，
也使企業更難單靠模型服務取得理想利潤。對投資人而言，9 月最重要的訊號不是單一產品的表現，

而是中國 AI 已進入需要同時回答算力效率、商業變現與資本回收的階段。
從整體結構來看，中國具備電力、製造基礎及廣泛應用場景，有利於 AI 以較低成本進入企業營
運與日常服務；但部署成本較低，不代表產業獲利便能同步提高。當業者以低價爭取用戶，使用量可
以迅速累積，收入卻可能跟不上基礎設施、研發及營運支出。以特定平台的觀察為例，DeepSeek 曾
取得可觀的代幣使用量，但相應支出占比明顯較低；這不能代表中國 AI 產業的整體財務表現，卻凸
顯「有人使用」與「願意付費」之間仍有距離。未來產業能否改善獲利，取決於企業是否找到具持續
需求的應用、建立合理收費方式，並避免同質化服務陷入反覆降價。
展望 10 月 ， 中國AI 的成長空間仍在 ， 但市場衡量進展的標準應由使用規模 ， 逐步轉向投入產出。
若 AI 更深入企業工作流程，相關需求有望延續；然而，需求增加後，業者仍須證明服務品質、運算
效率與客戶付費能同步改善 。 晶片限制使算力使用效率更加重要 ， 價格競爭則使收入品質成為另一項
考驗。Vercel 資料顯示，8 月開放權重模型在其平台占代幣使用量 56%，支出占比卻為 14%，說明

高使用量未必對應高支出；10 月宜觀察企業是否開始披露更清楚的付費需求與成本改善，而非只比
較流量或模型排名。
投資策略方面，對中國 AI 宜維持關注成長、審慎評估獲利的態度，避免把產業滲透率提高，直
接等同於所有企業的投資價值提升。評估重點可放在三個整體問題：第一，持續增加的算力與研發投
入，能否換來足夠的收入；第二，低價競爭下，企業能否留住付費客戶並維持合理利潤；第三，AI
應用能否從試用走向長期採購。具備穩定需求、成本控制能力及清楚收費模式的企業，較有機會在產
業整合中建立優勢。整體而言，中國 AI 並非缺少使用場景，而是仍須證明大規模使用可以轉化為可
持續的獲利，這將是 10 月觀察產業與相關市場表現的核心。

科技類股：AI 應用加速落地，光通訊與 Agent 入口迎來成長機會
AI 產業的成長主軸正由模型訓練延伸至推論與實際應用 ， 讓軟體服務與硬體建設形成相互帶動的
循環。當 AI 不再只回答問題，而能協助搜尋資訊、安排活動及完成跨平台任務，使用頻率與運算需
求有望提高。Meta 於 9 月推出個人 AI 助理 Muse，透過應用程式及 WhatsApp 接觸既有用戶，積
極爭取 AI 服務入口。對產業而言，Agent 普及不僅意味新的服務形式，也可能擴大雲端運算與資料
傳輸需求，使應用端的創新成為基礎設施升級的推力。投資機會可從使用者入口延伸至相關供應鏈。

在硬體端，AI 推論與多步驟任務若持續增加，資料中心內部的資料交換將更頻繁，高速且節能的
連接能力因而更具價值 。 光通訊除支援資料中心之間的傳輸 ， 也逐步朝機櫃及晶片附近的互連場景發
展 ， 拓展供應商的產品空間 。Lumentum 指出 ，AI 運算對速度與頻寬的要求提升 ， 帶動光連結需求，
並已在營收展望中反映相關成長 。 高速光模組與新型互連方案也持續推進 。 雖然部分新技術仍須經過
客戶導入與量產驗證，但既有產品需求與後續技術升級有望接續，讓光通訊成為 AI 基礎設施中值得
持續關注的環節。
在應用端，Muse 代表 AI 從被動回覆走向主動協助執行任務的嘗試。若 Agent 能替使用者完成
搜尋、比較、預訂與付款等流程，便利性提升有望促進使用黏著度，並為支付、交易與服務串接平台
創造新的合作機會。跨平台操作可能由單一入口整合，促使各產業重新思考客戶服務。 「Muse 對各
產業的影響」圖可呈現商業模式轉變的可能方向。隨著更多服務接入 Agent 生態系，使用情境有望
持續擴展；後續若任務完成率、使用留存及付費轉換逐步改善，應用端便有機會形成更明確的收入來

源，並進一步支持運算與傳輸投資。
投資策略方面，建議以「應用入口與基礎設施並重」掌握 AI 從訓練走向推論的成長機會。硬體
方面，可優先關注已切入主要客戶供應鏈、具備高速光模組與新型互連產品布局，且訂單能見度較佳
的業者。應用方面，則可追蹤具備用戶基礎、服務整合能力與清楚收費模式的平台，觀察 Agent 能
否從新產品熱度轉化為持續使用與付費需求 。 配置上可先以業績較明確的基礎設施業者為核心 ， 再逐
步增加應用平台的布局，兼顧當前需求與後續成長空間。整體而言，AI 應用愈深入日常生活與企業
流程 ， 入口平台與光通訊供應鏈愈有機會相互受惠 ； 選股關鍵仍是找出能將需求成長轉化為實際業績
的企業。

拉美市場：10 月巴西總統大選將登場，政治不確定性影響市場表現
9 月拉美市場維持震盪整理 。 一方面 ， 拉美各國通膨數據普遍低於市場預期 ，有利市場降息預期；
另一方面，巴西總統大選選情持續拉鋸，政治不確定性升溫則讓投資人保持觀望。展望 10 月，隨巴
西大選投票日逼近，政治因素仍可能使拉美市場短線波動加劇。
受到美伊衝突帶動油價上漲影響，市場原本擔心燃料與運輸成本增加，會讓拉美物價再度大幅上
漲，進而迫使拉美央行升息。然而，高盛分析指出，拉美市場目前的通膨率仍受到控制，當地經濟承
受能源漲價的能力 ， 比市場原先擔心的情況更好 。 其中一個重要原因是 ， 新興市場政府透過燃油補貼 、

稅制安排與零售價格管制，讓消費者實際感受到的價格漲幅相對有限。此外，上半年全球農產品供給
較為充裕，使食品價格漲幅偏低，也抵銷了部分能源漲價帶來的影響。由於拉美市場實際通膨壓力小
於原先市場預期，央行進一步大幅升息的必要性也隨之降低，高盛因此認為，市場目前對多數拉美經
濟體未來一至兩年的升息預期過於悲觀，並預期拉美各國央行在 2027 年較可能重新轉向降息。
不過，拉美多國經濟出現放緩跡象，亦可能壓抑股市上漲空間。智利央行於 9 月會議維持政策利
率 4.50%不變 ， 並將2026 年經濟成長預測下修至 0.25%至 0.75%， 低於6 月預估的 1.0%至 1.75%。
智利央行指出，第二季內需成長放緩，勞動市場及信心指標同步轉弱，加上燃料價格上漲侵蝕企業與
家庭所得，均對經濟活動形成壓力。巴西經濟亦出現放緩跡象，巴西央行於 9 月 16 日一致決議調降
Selic 政策利率 25 個基點至 13.75%，並指出景氣循環產業動能轉弱，且整體與核心通膨均已回落至
目標區間上限以下，為進一步降息提供空間。不過，巴西央行並未承諾下次會議是否將繼續降息，而

是強調將視未來經濟與通膨數據逐次評估。
另一方面，巴西總統大選進入白熱化階段則是當前影響市場最主要的因子。市場民調顯示，相較
7 月時現任總統魯拉在民調中普遍領先競爭者波索納洛約 5 至 8 個百分點 ，在 9 月的民調雙方差距已
縮小至 2 個百分點以內，選情不確定性仍高。若選舉最終由魯拉勝選，其支持財政擴張的政策，將可
能使市場對於巴西財政赤字問題的擔憂加劇，進而使巴西股市面臨較大壓力；而若波索納洛勝選，市
場普遍預期其能提出改善投資人信心的財政政策 ， 為巴西央行創造更多降息空間 ， 有利後續巴西股市
上行。
整體而言，儘管市場對拉美 各國當前升息預期可能過於悲觀，但目前拉美多國經濟成長同步放
緩，加上巴西總統大選選情膠著，選後財政政策方向不確定性高，在考量大選及政策方向不確定性的
狀況下，短線拉美市場波動恐將加劇。

能源類股：全球石油庫存持續下降，但中東石油出口已出現回升
9 月西德州原油期貨價格受美伊衝突升溫影響，一度於月中重新站上每桶 100 美元，其後隨衝突
趨緩，油價漲幅逐步收斂，月底約回落至90 美元附近，能源類股表現亦大致跟隨油價走勢。展望10
月，近期中東石油供應恢復速度不如預期，加上庫存持續下降，預期油價下檔仍具支撐。然而，市場
數據顯示，高油價及供應受阻已同步抑制石油需求，且中東各國原油出口持續回升，後續供應緊張情
況有望逐步改善。因此，雖然中東地緣政治風險仍將對油價形成支撐，但隨美伊重啟外交接觸，油價
進一步大幅上漲的空間預期相對有限。

受到美伊談判僵持影響，國際能源署（IEA）與美國能源資訊署（EIA）在 9 月月報均將中東石油
供應恢復的預期時間延後。IEA 將中東石油供應全面恢復的預期時間延至 2027 年，並將 2026 年全
球石油供應減幅，由上月預估的每日減少 430 萬桶擴大至 570 萬桶；EIA 則將中東石油供給恢復至
衝突前水準的時間，由 2027 年初延至第 2 季。
此外 ，受到中東供應受阻與油價偏高影響 ，IEA 亦將 2026 年全球石油需求預測下修每日 94 萬桶 ，
預估全年需求將較去年減少每日 250 萬桶，EIA 則預估全年石油需求將減少約每日 170 萬桶，石油
輸出國組織（OPEC）也將今年需求由上月預估的每日增加 60 萬桶下修至 40 萬桶。
受此影響，全球石油庫存持續下降。IEA 估計，8 月全球可觀察石油庫存減少 9,500 萬桶，自 2
月底以來已累計下降 5.07 億桶，顯示市場仍持續動用庫存填補缺口。EIA 也預期全球石油庫存將持
續下降至年底，因此將2026 年下半年布蘭特原油均價預測，由上月約每桶82 美元上調至 90 美元；

此外 ，隨著 2027 年中東出口與產量逐步恢復 ，IEA 預估明年全年布蘭特原油均價將回落至 74 美元。
另一方面，中東原油出口則已經出現回升跡象。路透社 9 月 28 日引述 Kpler 初步資料指出，沙
烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋 9 月原油出口均出現上升 ， 帶動中東主要產油國當月原油日均出口量回升至每日
1,280 萬桶，創下 2 月戰爭爆發以來最高水準。其中，沙烏地阿拉伯9 月原油日均出口預估將達每日
540 萬桶，較 8 月的 244.6 萬桶增加逾一倍。由於沙國東西向輸油管線先前因攻擊受損，沙烏地計
畫將部分原先經紅海延布港出口的原油 ， 轉往波斯灣沿岸港口裝運 ， 帶動拉斯坦努拉港9 月日均出貨
量預估由 8 月的每日 92.9 萬桶增至 360 萬桶。
整體而言，中東供應恢復時程延後，加上全球石油庫存持續下降，短期仍有利支撐油價。然而，
石油需求預估同步下修 ， 且沙烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋原油出口均已逐步回升 ，後續石油供應緊張程度可
望有所緩解。整體而言，儘管油價仍受中東地緣風險支撐，但隨美國期中選舉接近，近期美伊亦重啟

外交接觸，市場對原油供應中斷的擔憂可望逐步降溫，油價進一步大幅上漲空間相對有限。

### Page 10

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
黃金類股：升息預期攀升壓抑金價表現；財富配置及央行買盤支撐中長期需求
近期黃金價格表現較為疲弱，主要受到市場升息預期持續攀升，帶動美債殖利率走高，增加持有
黃金的機會成本，對於利率較為敏感的黃金 ETF 需求因此受到壓抑。然而，高盛研究指出，聯準會
近期決議升息雖然延長了市場高利率環境時間，但並不會進一步墊高利率終點 ， 加上全球央行持續購
金，以及投資人從財富配置角度增加黃金配置，均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。
高盛報告指出，聯準會於 9 月升息一碼，增加持有黃金的機會成本，使對利率敏感的黃金ETF 需
求下滑 ， 高盛因此將2026 年底金價預估由每盎司 4,900 美元下修至 4,650 美元 。 然而 ， 高盛也預估，

聯準會將於 2027 年 9 月至 2028 年 3 月期間降息三碼，使政策利率最終回到 3.25%至 3.5%區間，
故儘管聯準會 9 月升息延長了高利率環境，但實際並未墊高利率終點，因此來自黃金 ETF 的賣壓僅
會延後金價上漲時程，但並未改變高盛對黃金的偏多看法。
另一方面，央行買盤仍是金價支撐核心。高盛9 月報告預估，全球央行近三個月經季節調整後的
購金速度已上升至每月 91 噸，遠高於 2022 年前每月 17 噸的平均水準。高盛指出，這股需求主要
來自分散外匯儲備的考量，在 2022 年俄羅斯央行資產遭美國凍結後，各國央行更加重視儲備資產遭
制裁或凍結的風險，相較於對利率敏感的 ETF 資金，央行基於儲備分散而進行的購金，不易因短期
利率變動而轉向，因此即使聯準會升息，央行買盤仍有利支撐金價表現。
除了央行買盤外，花旗也從全球財富配置的角度看好黃金。花旗指出，全球每年新增黃金礦產供
應規模相當有限，若全球家庭額外將 0.1%的財富配置於黃金，就相當於約 4,459 噸黃金購買量，也

等於 1.2 年的新增礦產供應量。換言之，即使全球投資人僅小幅提高黃金配置比重，也可能創造遠超
過新增供給的需求。由於黃金供給短期內難以快速增加，市場往往需要透過金價上漲，吸引既有持有
人釋出黃金以滿足新增需求，因此資產配置需求增加可能對金價產生顯著推升影響。
不過，花旗也指出，黃金新增供給有限的特性雖有助於放大需求對金價的推升效果，但同時也可
能提高金價出現較劇烈修正的風險。花旗估計，2022 年以來的金價上漲已使既有黃金持有人的帳面
財富增加約 13 兆美元，若持有人將其中約 1 兆美元轉回股票、債券或現金，所形成的黃金賣壓便可
能超過目前全部實體黃金需求，故當持有人集中獲利了結時，金價也可能出現較劇烈的修正。
整體而言，在市場升息預期持續攀升的情況下，短期黃金ETF 需求可能受到壓抑，金價仍可能面
臨震盪 。 然而 ， 全球央行基於外匯儲備分散需求持續購金 ， 加上投資人從財富配置角度提高黃金配置 ，
均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。

身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：聯準會升息穩定通膨預期，凸顯中天期美債與投資級債吸引力
美國 9 月聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 全票通過 ， 將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調 25 個基點至
3.75%–4.00%。最新經濟預測同步將 2026 年核心 PCE 上修至 3.4%、GDP 成長率上修至 2.3%，且
全面上修 2026 至 2027 年政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示年底前仍有一次升息空間。9 月聯準會採取
預防性升息，主要是在避免通膨預期鬆動並維持政策公信力，確立高利率維持更久的政策基調。
美國政府於 9 月 30 日公布第二季國內生產毛額(GDP)終值，經季節調整後成長年率由 1.5%上修

至 2.2%，高於經濟學家預估的維持不變。第一季 GDP 季增年率也由 2.1%上修至 2.5%。其中，占
美國經濟活動逾 6 成的消費支出，第二季成長率由 3.4%上修至 3.8%，顯示美國消費動能強勁；元
大投顧指出，美國 8 月核心資本財訂單月增率加速至 1.6%，其中初級金屬、機械及電機設備訂單均
呈現增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成
長形成穩健貢獻。在消費與企業動能表現強勁下，使市場重新評估美國經濟對通膨黏性的影響。
彭博 10 月 1 日的報告指出，在 Market Pulse 調查的 173 名受訪者，超過一半的人預測美國 30
年期公債殖利率在年底將達到 6%。儘管伊朗戰爭和高借貸成本持續影響 ， 美國經濟仍維持強勁成長 ，
這種韌性加劇了通膨和進一步升息的⾵險；人工智慧基礎設施建設競賽引發了企業發債的狂潮 ，推升
長天期債券發行量，全球企業今年以來已發行超過 4000 億美元的債券，用於技術投資，而其中大部
分債券發行於美國。另外，各國政府持續的預算赤字，美國聯邦政府債務規模首次突破 40 兆美元，

美國政府需要擴大發債量 ， 投資人要求更高的利率來購買債券等因素 ，皆為推升美國長天期殖利率上
行的原因。因此，長天期債券後續表現仍須持續觀察利率走勢、債券供需變化及通膨發展。
展望 10 月，由於美國通膨仍高於聯準會 2%的目標，2026 年底有再升息一碼的可能，使得短
天期利率下行空間有限。在政策利率可能維持高檔、長天期殖利率又受到供給面壓力推升的環境下，
當前的債券配置應以「息收防禦」與「存續期間控管」為核心。隨著美國公債利率上行，彭博美國投
資級債指數平均剩餘到期期限持續下降 ， 存續期間縮短有助降低利率變動對債券價格的影響 。美國債
市投資建議重點配置中天期美國公債與投資級公司債 。中天期美國公債與投資級公司債不僅能鎖定具
吸引力的收益率水準，也能兼顧存續期間風險控管，降低利率波動對投資組合淨值的衝擊，在當前環
境下有助於兼顧收益與風險控制。

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We believe an improving product mix, driven by rising 
Nvidia’s CPU contribution, to be a key near-term share price catalyst.

What’s the impact: Near-term update. After Kinsus released its August 
preliminary results on 23 September, we note that we had underestimated 
the 3Q26E gross margin expansion on product optimisation and price hike 
scale. The results imply a GM of 33% in August (vs. 26% in 2Q26). We 
therefore lift our 3Q-4Q26E gross margin to 33.2-35.1% (from 28.9-30.3%) 
to reflect an improving product mix, including greater CPU substrate 
contribution. We believe the gross margin of high-end chipset substrate 
may be c.50%, supporting margin expansion. We also expect a c.10% QoQ 
price hike for both BT and ABF substrate in 3Q26, above Kinsus’ guidance 
of a high-single-digit% QoQ, making these substrates the other growth 
driver. We see further gross margin expansion in 2027-28 on the ongoing 
price hike trend and increasing high-end ABF substrate product mix.

Business outlook. We believe several favourable trends lie ahead. First, 
we see a rising contribution from high-end GPU and CPU ABF substrates. 
We believe Nvidia’s CPU currently accounts for 5-10% of total revenue, 
with Kinsus holding a 70-80% market share. We also expect Kinsus to 
acquire a teens% market share in AMD’s Venice, which should gradually 
ramp up from 4Q26. These will likely become key growth drivers of the ABF 
substrate segment in 2027, in our view. We believe the ramp-up progress 
for GPU substrate for these two clients is slightly slower than that for CPU. 
Second, we believe Kinsus will benefit from the rising BT substrate demand 
for optical transceivers. In our view, migration to on-board optics (OBO) and 
NPO/CPO will require ABF substrate beneath the optics engine (OE) in 
OBO; the OE and IC will need a bigger ABF substrate as we move to the 
NPO/CPO generation. As such, we expect the total ABF substrate demand 
to increase with rising adoption of optics in AI servers. Lastly, we expect the 
adoption of multiple-core ABF from 2028 to improve the rigidity and 
transmission performance before the official launch of ceramic and glass-
core substrate, which we expect to increase the ABF TAM as well.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E earnings by 19-29% to 
reflect the better-than-expected gross margin in August. We reaffirm our 
Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP to TWD1,580, based on an unchanged 
target PER of 36x (high end of 3-year-trading range of 10-36x), applied to 
the 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key downside risks: weaker-than-
expected demand for memory, PCs, gaming GPUs or AI servers.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 27-57% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely on our more bullish view on its gross margin expansion.

6 October 2026

3Q26 results preview: several favourable trends ahead

➢ Rising high-end ABF substrate likely to drive blended ASP increase   
➢ Multicore and optics likely to be the next growth drivers 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising 12M TP to TWD1,580

Company profile  
Kinsus was founded in 2000 as a subsidiary of Asustek. The company mainly focus on niche ball grid 
array (BGA) substrates to avoid price competition among mainstream products like graphics card and 
chipsets, and it has become top-3 flip chip manufacturer in global. Besides, its manufacturing sites are 
based in Taiwan and mainland China.

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Kinsus's leadership team comprises experienced professionals dedicated to steering the
company towards sustainable growth. This diverse board composition ensures a comprehensive
range of expertise and perspectives, contributing to Kinsus’s strategic decision-making and
corporate governamce.
Capital management 2
Kinsus maintains a robust financial framework, ensuring the availability of resources necessary
for operational efficiency and strategic investments. The company's financial health is regularly
disclosed through annual reports, providing transparency to stakeholders.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company adheres to stringent corporate governance policies to manage related party
transactions, ensuring transparency and compliance with regulatory standards. The company
discloses such transactions in its financial reports, maintaining accountability and stakeholder
trust. For instance, the company's annual general meeting documents provide details on related
party transactions, ensuring transparency and accountability.
S Supply chainmanagement 1
Kinsus is committed to sustainable supply chain practices, emphasizing environmental, social,
and governance considerations. The company has established comprehensive ESG policies,
including commitments to labor standards, environmental health and safety, ethics, and conflict-
free minerals. These policies guide the company's interactions with suppliers and partners,
promoting sustainability throughout the supply chain.
E Data security 3
The company recognizes the importance of information security in its operations. As part of its
corporate governance framework, Kinsus is likely to implement standard data protection
practices to safeguard sensitive information and ensure compliance with relevant regulations.
Stakeholders are encouraged to consult the company's official communications or contact the
company directly for detailed information on data security policies.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Update
King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Preliminary August Profit and
September Monthly Sales (-31%
MoM/+196% YoY)
Sentiment to stay soft in near term but 2027 outlook is
promising.
King Slide announced its preliminary profit for August, per TWSE regulatory
requirements:
• Revenue was NT$6,466mn (+1% MoM/+344% YoY), 33% of our estimate for
3Q26 of NT$19,555mn (+81% QoQ/+348% YoY) and 39% of consensus' NT
$16,755mn (+55% QoQ/+284% YoY).

• Pretax income was NT$4,826mn (+216% YoY; pretax margin at 74.6%), 29%
of our 3Q26 pretax income estimate of NT$16,377mn (margin at 83.7%) and
34% of consensus' NT$14,094mn (margin at 84.1%).
• Net income was NT$3,819mn (+212% YoY; EPS NT$40.07; NPM 59.1%), 30%
of our 3Q26 net income estimate of NT$12,938mn (EPS NT$135.76; NPM
66.2%) and 34% consensus' NT$11,118mn (EPS NT$114.38; NPM 66.4%).
September monthly sales
• September sales of NT$4,464mn (-31% MoM/+196% YoY), driving 3Q26

revenue to NT$17,338mn (+60% QoQ/+297% YoY), were short of our
estimate of NT$19,555mn (+81% QoQ/+348% YoY) and but ahead of the
consensus forecast of NT$16,755mn (+55% QoQ/+284% YoY).
Our view:
• We think the sequential decline in September  revenue was driven by slower
momentum for some projects, which entered the later part of their product
cycles. While sentiment should stay soft in the near term, we think the
outlook into 2027 is still promising as customers start new product cycles.

• We maintain our view that King Slide's competitiveness in the comprehensive
patent portfolio and strong execution are entrenched and accentuated amid
fast generation migration in AI applications.
• Stay OW with PT of NT$15,000 (30x 2027e P/E).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it better captures King Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our target mul-
tiple is justified by an operating profit CAGR of 77% seen for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.4
vs. the average of 1.2 over 2010-24, when the stock was trading at 17-18x P/E with an operating

profit CAGR of 14%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI capex
n Stronger server demand
n Faster market share gains
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Weaker server demand
n Sluggish kitchen furniture demand
n Competition leading to slower share gains.

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Document 106: 9 月会合以降に日銀を取り巻く状況が変わった印象

Source PDF: all report pdf/9 月会合以降に日銀を取り巻く状況が変わった印象.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 7 日(水)

1/3 
9 月会合以降に日銀を取り巻く状況が変わった印象  
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・時事通信は10月６日付で「日銀が『基調的な物価上昇率』について、２％程度に到達したと
判断する方向で検討に入ることが５日、分かった」と報じた。記事内には10月29・30日開催
の金融政策決定会合で取りまとめる展望レポートで、そうした認識が記述されるとしてい
る。

・筆者はこの報道が現実になる可能性が高いと判断している。日銀は予てから基調的物価上昇
率が２％に到達する時期を「2026年度後半から2027年度」と予想してきたので（2026年度後
半にあたる）10月にそうした認識を示さないと、ハト派的な印象を与えてしまうことにな
る。

・既に日銀の政策態度は基調的物価上昇率を２％に「引き上げる」段階から、２％以上に「上
振れさせない」段階に移行している。局面が変化した理由としては、やはり原油高とそれに
対する企業の価格設定行動の積極化が大きいだろう。従来、原油高は企業収益を圧迫するこ
とで、賃上げを阻害し、個人消費の停滞に繋がってきた。そうした環境下で内需は拡大しな
いため、原油高は短期的にインフレ要因となっても、やや長い目でみるとデフレ的な色彩を
帯びていた。もっとも、2026年春先以降の原油高局面では、政府の手厚い支援もあり、企業
・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000 円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPY は先行き12 ヶ月 155 円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12 月に 1.5％とした後、27 年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FED はFF 金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/3 
は価格転嫁を積極的に進めた。企業物価指数は４月に前月比＋2.8％と即座に上昇し、前年比
伸び率は８月時点でも＋7.6％と高止まりしている。日銀短観（６月および９月調査）では１
年後の販売価格見通しが急伸した。こうした早期かつ大幅な価格転嫁の進展によって、企業
収益は傷みにくくなっている。このことは、名目賃金と名目消費が伸びやすい構図にあるこ
とを浮き彫りにしている。

・日銀が、基調的物価上昇率が２％に到達したとの認識を示すのであれば、それは利上げペー
スの加速を自ら正当化することになる。さすがに10月の利上げはビハインド・ザ・カーブに
陥ったことを認めたと市場参加者に受け止められる恐れがあるため、見送られると予想する
が、（前回利上げから約３ヶ月が経過する）12月会合では利上げが決定されるだろう。そう
なれば、３～４ヶ月に一度の利上げが標準ペースとなることが市場参加者に広く意識される
だろう。

・利上げを巡っては、経済指標に加えて「政府との距離感」を考慮する必要があり、予想は難
儀であった。しかしながら、ここ数週間で情勢は変化したように感じる。９月25日に片山さ
つき財務相が「官邸とも相談の上、私から初めて申し上げるが、先日の日米首脳会談では、
為替について、トランプ大統領から円安への懸念が示された」と発言したことを皮切りに、
同日には城内経済再生相が「アベノミクス的な意味でのリフレーション政策は、もうそうい
う局面は終わっている」とした。また10月５日に公表された９月30日の経済財政諮問会議の
議事要旨では、民間議員である若田部昌澄氏が「デフレではない状況になりつつある現状で
リフレ政策が終焉を迎えているというのは当然のことだ」との見解を示し、さらに９月の金
融政策決定会合で反対票を投じた佐藤綾野日銀政策委員は６日までに共同通信の単独インタ

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告

りません。 
3/3 
ビューに応じて「（政策金利を）段階的に調整していく方針には賛成している」とした。９
月18日会合における「主な意見」で内閣府の出席者（おそらく城内大臣）が利上げに消極的
と読める発言をしていたことと整合しないが、それは9月18日から25日の間に政府の態度が変
化した可能性が指摘できる。

・10月会合までに円安が進む、あるいは原油高等の要因によってＦｅｄが利上げに踏み切る事
態となれば、日銀も追随する可能性があるものの、そうした状況に至らなければ、10月は
「既往の利上げ効果を見極めたい」などとして利上げを見送るだろう。

・なお、これらに鑑みて、筆者は2027年７月の審議委員人事が中立ないしはタカ派的な人選に
なると予想する。高田委員と田村委員に考え方の近い人物に白羽の矢が立ち、リフレ派が送
り込まれることはないと考えている。利上げに積極的な２名の後任人事にリフレ派と目され
る人物が浮上した場合、金融市場では長期金利上昇や円安の反応が予想される。

Document 142: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

(373220) 
LG 에너지솔루션 
2026.10.12

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

보상금 제외해도 호실적 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 530,000원(상향) 
 현재주가 401,000원(10/8)

 [투자의견] 당사는 LG에너지솔루션에 대해 실적 추정치 상향을 반영하여 목
표주가를 530,000원으로 13% 상향하고, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 
3Q26부터 ESS 물량 증가에 따른 가동률 상승 효과가 본격적으로 실적에 
반영되기 시작했으며, 4Q26에도 AMPC 제외 흑자 기조를 이어갈 전망이
다. 전사 가동률은 3분기 65%까지 상승한 것으로 파악되며, 이에 따른 펀
더멘털 개선이 본격화되고 있다고 판단한다. 
 [3Q26 잠정 실적 Review] 매출액 9.6조원(+28%qoq, +59%yoy), 영업이
익 7,560억원(+567%qoq, +26%yoy)을 기록하며 당사 추정을 큰 폭으로 
상회했다. 북미 생산보조금(AMPC)을 제외한 매출액은 9.2조원, 영업이익은 
3,391억원(영업이익률 3.7%)으로, 2 분기(-1,277억원, -1.7%) 대비 흑자 
전환에 성공했다. ① 원통형은 테슬라향 물량 호조에 힘입어 영업이익 
2,010억원(영업이익률 8.1%)을 달성한 것으로 추정된다. ② EV는 유럽 폴
란드 공장의 가동률 상승과 GM 얼티엄의 8월 가동 개시로 매출이 증가했
다. 여기에 완성차 업체 보상금 3,000억원이 반영되며 영업이익 1,090억
원(영업이익률 2.5%)을 기록한 것으로 추정된다. ③ ESS는 매출액이 50% 
이상 급증하며 실적 서프라이즈를 견인했다. AMPC 포함 영업이익은 4,460
억원(영업이익률 16.0%)으로 추정된다. 2 분기 매출 성장률 +17%qoq에 
이어 3분기 성장세가 더욱 가팔라졌다는 점이 긍정적이다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 8.0조원(-17%qoq, +30%yoy), 영업이익 6,790
억원(-10%qoq, 흑전yoy)으로 추정한다. 4 분기 실적은 ESS 수익성 개선 
속도와 완성차 업체 보상금 반영 규모에 따라 변동할 전망이다. 동사는 기존 
가이던스를 통해 하반기 북미 ESS 생산량이 상반기 대비 2배 증가할 것으
로 제시한 바 있다. 3분기 ESS는 AMPC를 제외하더라도 양호한 수익성을 
기록한 것으로 추정된다. 향후 팩 생산 병목 해소와 북미 공장 가동 안정화
에 따른 고정비 부담 완화가 이어지며 추가적인 손익 개선이 가능할 전망이
다. EV 역시 GM 얼티엄 생산 확대와 유럽 공장 가동률 상승이 지속되며 
AMPC 제외 적자 규모를 점진적으로 축소할 것으로 예상한다. 
 [투자포인트] 9월 미시간 랜싱 공장의 개소식이 개최됐다. 랜싱 공장은 당초 
EV용 파우치 배터리 생산거점으로 계획됐으나 ESS 중심으로 역할이 재편
됐으며, 내년에는 10GWh 이상의 각형 LFP 생산라인이 추가될 예정이다. 
2027년에는 신규 대규모 증설보다 기존 북미 생산거점의 가동률 상승과 
ESS 생산량 확대를 통한 수익성 개선이 핵심이 될 전망이다. 북미 전력망 
접속 지연과 AI 데이터센터의 부하 변동성 확대에 따라 Stand-alone ESS, 
BTM, UPS, BBU 수요가 증가하면서 신규 수주도 지속 확대될 것으로 예상
한다. 특히 동사의 Non-PFE 요건 충족과 SI· O&M을 아우르는 End-to-
End 솔루션 역량이 북미 ESS 시장에서 경쟁력으로 작용할 전망이다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

Editorial 
A Fed credibility-induced bounce  
Strategy summary:  The Fed’s unanimous decision to hike 
rates this week for the first time since 2023 , and pencil in 
another hike by December, confirms our expectations – flagged 
in the last two weeklies - of a central bank willing to establish its 
independence against political pressure. The Fed’s moderately 
hawkish pivot is helping restore its inflation-fighting credentials. 
The BoJ also hiked rates this week. It is the first time on record 
all three major central banks – Fed, ECB and BoJ – have hiked 
in the same month , as they respond primarily to a resurgence 
in energy-driven inflation amid tight job markets.  
We expect the Fed to hike once more by year-end to 4.0-4.25% 
and follow up with another hike in H1 2027 to 4.25 -4.5%. With 
the revised estimates, we revise up our US government bond 
yield forecasts. Nevertheless, with Fed policy uncertainty 
easing, we see an opportunity to use the equity market dip to 
add exposure amid solid corporate earnings.  
Fed moves to restore credibility:  The Fed’s policy pivot this 
week followed Chair Warsh’s hawkish Jackson Hole speech 
and recent economic data prints, namely: a pick -up in job 
creation and inflation over the summer and a sharp rebound in 
oil prices due to the re -escalation of the Middle E ast conflict. 
Given the unanimous Fed decision to hike rates this week, we 
expect two more hikes by  June 2027 on the back of  robust 
growth amid strong AI investments and a tight labour market, 
keeping inflation elevated. We expect this pivot to help the Fed 
rebuild credibility as an inflation fighter.  
Policy outlook less aggressive than market pricing : The 
updated Fed policy rate estimates are lower compared with 
money market pricing of three more rate hikes by June 2027 
since we expect disinflation to take hold by Q2 2027 as the 
impact of oil prices and tariffs fade. We believe policy rates can 
return to the 4.0–4.25% range by end-2027, with the possibility 
of cuts in H2 2027 as inflation pressures ease.  
Upgrading near-term US rates and USD estimates : Based 
on these revised estimates, we also upgrade our 3-month target 
for the US 10 -year government bond yield to 5.0 -5.25%. We 
also revise up our 12 -month target for the US 10 -year bond 
yield to 4.75-5.0% (see page 4).  
Buying the equity dip as focus turns to fundamentals: Fed 
hikes typically do not hurt equities over the medium-term when 
accompanied by strong earnings growth. We estimate a 25bps 
rise in Fed rates (the discount rate) would impact the S&P500 
index and global equities by 3 -4%. However, we expect the 
headwind from higher rates to be offset by strong earnings 
growth, driving positive performance for global equities.  
Furthermore, a hawkish Fed is largely priced in and we expect 
the Fed to deliver fewer rate hikes than currently priced by 
markets. Meanwhile, we believe concerns over AI industry 
leaders slowing the pace of development are overdone. Hence, 
we maintain our preference for a diversified group of large cap 
technology and semiconductor industry leaders (see page 5-6). 
BoJ hikes; modestly bullish JPY : The BoJ hiked rates by 
25bps to a three-decade high of 1.25%, as expected, but the 7-
2 split vote led to renewed JPY weakness on concerns it may 
slow the pace of further hikes. We remain constructive on JPY 
amid narrowing rate differential vs peers as the BoJ potentially 
hikes rates by 25bps once per quarter until Q2 2027 to curb 
rising domestic wage-driven inflation pressure . Any sign of BoJ 
slowing the pace of hikes could lead to renewed JPY weakness, 
fuelling inflationary pressures (see page 7).  
BoE holds; rangebound GBP: The BoE held rates this week, 
bucking the hiking trend among peers, as weakening UK job 
and housing markets offset rebound in energy-driven inflation. 
We expect BoE to deliver fewer rate hikes than the 100bps of 
increase currently priced by markets  as the job market slows 
further. This is likely to keep GBP rangebound (see page 7).  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions 
  Where do you see US government bond  yields following 
the Fed’s 16 September decision to hike interest rates? 
Our view: The US 10-year government bond yield is likely to 
retest the 5.00-5.25% range in the near term. We favour 3-7-year 
maturity bond s in t he US, preferring credit over government 
bonds. We are looking for an opportunity to extend duration 
approaching Q2 2027. 
Rationale: Over the next few months, we expect 2- and 30-year US 
government bond yields to remain rangebound, but the 10-year yield 
is expected to rebound up to the 5.00-5.25% range. The reasons for 
this include: 1) oil prices staying elevated and potentially rise further 
due to the fluid geopolitical situation in the Middle East, with Saudi 
Arabia’s East-West oil pipeline shut, the Houthis now firmly in control 
of the Strait of Bab-al-Mandab, the Strait of Hormuz still closed and 
China returning as a buyer of crude oil in the market ; 2) despite 
notable AI leaders expressing the need to slow AI development, AI 
capex plans are showing no signs of slowing down, which continues 
to drive above-trend growth in the economy. This, in turn, also means 
AI-related heavy bond issuance  is expected to continue. These 
issuers typically issue long-dated tenors, and this competition for 
capital will also exert upward pressure on the US 10-year yields. 
We note that the US 10-year bond yield has room to move lower, 
driven by short-covering activities in the very near term as net-short 
positions had moved to extreme levels before th is week’s  Fed 
meeting. However, we think such downshifts would be short-lived 
and the central tendency for the 10-year yield would be to move back 
up to the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Meanwhile, we see US 30-year yields continuing to reflect the dire 
fiscal outlook for the US government. We expect US 2-year yields 
to continue trading in a range that reflects expectations of further 
federal funds rate hikes. For the record, we only expect two more 
rate hikes from the Fed into the middle of 2027 , while money 
markets are pricing in three more for the same period. 
Going into H2 2027, we expect oil prices to moderate (either 
through a resolution of the M iddle East conflict or a re-routing of oil 
supply chains)  and the inflationary effects from tariffs to fade. 
Inflation by then is likely be close to – if not at – the Fed’s target of 
2%. At this point, we see room for the Fed to contemplate rate 
cuts. US 2-year yields (the tenor that is most sensitive to Fed policy 
changes) will likely start to price th is in. With oil prices and inflation 
cooling, 10-year yields would also have room to come down. 
US 30-year yields, on the other hand, are expected to  remain 
elevated as the US fiscal deficit remains substantial next year and 
could become much worse than its already dire state today. As a 
result, the ‘term premium ’ may keep the ultra -long end elevated. 
Short of some unorthodox policy to artificially cap them lower, 30 -
year yields could continue to drift higher into H2 2027. 
We continue to favour the 3-7-year segment of the yield curve and 
prefer credit over government bond s. We see an opportunity for 
us to extend duration closer to Q2 2027. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO for Fixed Income and FX

We have updated our three- and 12-month outlook 
for US government bond yields

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Will the step -up in the  Fed’s risk-free rate trigger a re -
pricing of equities? 
Our view: We expect strong corporate earnings growth to offset 
the headwind of higher rates , driving positive performance for 
global equities. We remain Overweight global equities. 
Rationale: The Fed’s latest 25bps rate hike had been anticipated 
by the bond market, partly reflected in the bond yield  rise we have 
seen so far in 2026. Using the US 10-year government bond yield as 
a proxy for the risk -free rate, the yield has risen by 80-85bps this 
year. We estimate that each 25bps rise in the discount rate presents 
a valuation drag on global equities. On this basis, the rise in yields 
represents a potential 10-14% headwind for equities this year. 
However, the Fed’s hike reflects a solid expansion of US economic 
activity, resilient domestic spending and robust capital investment. 
This is consistent with the positive earnings revisions we have seen 
this year, with  2026 earnings growth projections now at 34% (and 
16% for 2027). Global equities are up 14% year-to-date (YTD), but 
even with a 14% valuation headwind from higher yields, we believe 
they can rise further, given strong earnings growth in 2026 and 2027. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist 
  What is the outlook for the US 10-year government bond 
yield? What is your tactical view on the DXY? 
Our view: We raise our three-month US 10 -year government 
bond yield forecast to the 5.00-5.25% range. Meanwhile, we see 
a near-term upward bias in the DXY after its recent downtrend, 
supported by recent Fed tightening and higher  short-term US 
bond yields.  
Rationale: The Fed 's decision  to raise the target range for the 
federal funds rate by 25bps to 3.75-4.00% validates the upside policy 
risks we flagged recently, following Fed Chair Warsh's Jackson Hole 
speech and a run of firmer US economic data. We expect growth 
to remain robust, the labour market  to remain tight and inflation to 
stay elevated, supporting one more hike before year-end 2026 and 
potentially another in H1 2027  – fewer than the three additional 
hikes currently priced in by money markets . For the US 10-year 
government bond yield, further policy tightening and lingering US 
fiscal concerns should keep yields elevated, but the Fed’s visible 
response to inflation should limit additional upward pressure, leaving 
the 10-year yield holding around the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Crucially, Warsh provided no forward guidance on further rate 
hikes. Markets had already priced in substantial additional rate hikes 
ahead of the Fed meeting, reducing the scope for further USD upside 
purely from rate repricing. Meanwhile, we expect further policy 
tightening from the ECB and the BoJ, while the RBA also remains 
biased towards further tightening. This should gradually narrow the 
US rate advantage and limit the extent of a DXY rebound. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Anthony Naab, CFA, Investment Strategist

Global equities’ earnings growth estimates have 
been revised higher this year, offsetting the 
headwind from higher rates

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in technology as AI lab 
CEOs call for a slowdown in frontier AI model development? 
Our view: We believe concerns over “pacing the frontier” are 
overdone. We maintain our preference to remain invested in 
and diversified across Big Tech and semiconductor leaders. 
Rationale: We believe it is prudent for investors to avoid 
overreacting to the recent calls for a slowdown in frontier AI model 
development, as they focus on responsible AI development, rather 
than halting AI model development or reducing spending. The global 
AI leadership  race, reinforced by the US and China’s  strategic 
ambitions, also makes a coordinated slowdown unlikely. 
While the near-term narrative may shift from AI training beneficiaries 
towards inference plays, hyperscalers continue to signal strong AI 
infrastructure commitments and improving monetisation, supporting 
our AI capex outlook. Rising AI adoption and agentic use cases 
should sustain inference demand, even if training activity moderates, 
benefiting custom AI chips, NAND flash memory and hyperscalers.  
We believe the decline in valuations  this year more than 
compensates for any downside risk to AI capex. Our Opportunistic 
idea on Global Semiconductors further reflects our conviction, with 
attractive valuations supporting selective additions during volatility. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  With ASEAN and UK equities outperforming AI-focused 
markets in H2 2026, is it worth adding to these markets? 
Our view: ASEAN and UK equities offer portfolio diversification, 
but we are Underweight both markets in favour of US and Asia 
ex-Japan (AxJ) equities, given their stronger earnings potential. 
Rationale: The ASEAN and UK markets have outperformed the 
MSCI ACWI over the past three months, but both have 
underperformed on a YTD basis. Financials is the largest sector in 
the ASEAN (53%) and the UK (28%) and provides steady earnings. 
However, both markets have minimal exposure to the technology 
sector (just 3% and 1% , respectively), ultimately dragging down 
growth. Hence, while defensive markets offer value by increasing 
diversification and reducing volatility in a portfolio, we maintain our 
Underweight allocation for ASEAN and the UK, as we see much 
stronger earnings potential elsewhere in the US and AxJ markets.  
We remain Overweight US and AxJ, supported by robust earnings 
growth, as  AI still has the potential to become a major economic 
growth driver . Our 12-month S&P500 Index forecast of 8,400  
implies low-teens upside, supported by a strong earnings backdrop 
of 25-30% earnings-per-share growth for 2026 and 15 -20% for 
2027. AxJ also boasts the strongest earnings growth among major 
markets, fuelled by the AI semiconductor super-cycle. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Why is it still appropriate to have a positive stance on US 
high-yield (HY) bonds? 
Our view: We remain opportunistically bullish on US HY bonds. 
Lower sensitivity to interest rate volatility, a below-trend default 
rate and a maturity wall pushed out to 2028-29 drive this view. 
Rationale: Recent interest rate volatility has highlighted a key 
structural advantage of US HY bonds: their higher coupon rates  
and shorter tenors relative to Developed Market (DM) government 
bonds and US investment-grade (IG) corporate bonds translate into 
a much lower sensitivity to interest rates, cushioning bond prices 
during yield backup episodes, where bond yields rise sharply. 
YTD, US HY has delivered 1.8% in total returns, outperforming DM 
government bonds and US IG corporate bonds by over 3% each on 
a relative basis. This outperformance was particularly evident during 
the recent back-up in bond yields, as inflation concerns re-emerged 
after renewed Middle East tensions – an environment where HY's 
lower rate sensitivity provided a relative cushion  even as broader 
fixed income markets came under pressure in absolute terms. On 
the fundamentals side, US HY corporate credit quality remains 
resilient. Default rates continue to track below long-run trend levels, 
and the maturity wall has been pushed out to 2028 -29, reducing 
near-term refinancing risk. Against a non -recessionary macro 
backdrop, we expect these credit fundamentals to hold, supporting 
the case for continued opportunistic exposure to the asset class.  
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Do the latest BoJ and BoE meetings alter your view on the 
central banks’ rate paths and their corresponding currencies? 
Our view: We remain constructive on the JPY and expect two 
BoJ rate hikes this year and potentially one hike per quarter in 
H1 2027 as policy normalisation continues. Meanwhile, the BoE 
held rates at 3.75%. We expect GBP/USD to stay rangebound 
within 1.32-1.36 over the next 1-3 months, with downside risk 
towards the lower end of our medium-term 1.30-1.36 range. 
Rationale: Persistent inflation, higher energy prices and earlier JPY 
weakness support further BoJ policy normalisation . Continued 
hiking should progressively narrow the US -Japan rate gap, reduce 
the attractiveness of JPY -funded carry trades and support  the JPY 
over the medium term towards the 153-155 range. However, with a 
September hike largely priced  in, future tightening ’s pace  will be 
more important for the JPY. Meanwhile, the BoE was split 6-3 in its 
decision to hold rates at 3.75% , with three members preferring a 
25bps hike, signalling elevated inflation risks. However, a weaker 
labour market is likely to limit the pace of BoE tightening. The US-
UK rate divergence is expected to favour the USD over the next 1-3 
months. Further BoE tightening (if inflation remains elevated ) could 
narrow the rate gap and provide support to the GBP later in the cycle. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist

We expect US HY bonds to continue delivering 
strong performance in the near term

Goldilocks 
We resume coverage of the PCB/CCL industry, focusing primarily on Google’s TPU supply 
chain, which we believe will deliver the strongest and most sustainable growth through 2028 
and beyond. We forecast aggregate TPU volumes to more than triple between 2025/26 and 
2028, far outpacing competing platforms. Critically, we project a surge in the content value 
of next-generation TPU ASICs. As Google integrates more advanced logic and HBM chiplets 
into a single compute module, we project TPU content value to increase five- to tenfold over 
the next three to four years.  Accordingly, we estimate that the addressable PCB content 
value alone could quadruple from 2026 to 2029. Equally vital is Google’s longstanding 
leadership in optical connectivity for its Superpods. The upcoming doubling of switch speeds 
and interconnect bandwidth will act as a secondary tailwind, further lifting PCB content 
value. Consequently, we expect the PCB/CCL market to experience continuous pricing power 
and a possibility of LTAs forming, mirro ring the supply dynamics seen in the memory 
industry. Within the key TPU vendor matrix, we resume coverage of Isu Petasys and EMC with 
BUY ratings and target prices of KRW 270k and TWD 10,000, respectively. We also initiate 
coverage of TTMI with a BUY rating and a target price of $236. Risks include end demand, 
execution, and US policy on AI data centre expansion. 
Why Google’s TPUs?  
We forecast TPU’s volume to more than triple to 12m units in 2028 (8.5mn in 2027) from 3.6mn 
in 2026, encompassing both in-house deployment and allocations to third-party AI labs via ASIC 
services. The strong volume is evident by its massive $520bn cloud service backlog orders in 
C2Q26. At the same time, TPU is expected to quadruple in TPU V9 (mid-28) vs V8 (2H26) and 
could double again in V10 (2029) as Google squeezes more chiplets into its ASIC designs. The 
complexity of design for PCB (upgrade to 50L+ or HDI) and CCL (upgrade to M8/M9 material or 
even PTFE) represents at least 50%-100% expansion in ASP in each generation. Furthermore, 
networking is expected to see a similar upgrade trend in PCB/CCL, as scale-up architectures 
and bandwidth requirement rise, especially from 400Gbps to 800G/1.6Tbps.    
LTA likely becomes new norm under strong demand and supply constraint  
Our supply chain survey indicated increasing discussion about long-term agreements (LTA) 
over the PCB and CCL supply chain with CSPs. Terms may be different from vendor to vendor, 
and most of them are still under discussion at this stage. However, those discussions send a 
clear message that end users are securing high-end PCB/CCL capacity by prepayment and LTAs 
for the next few years, because the overall industry may continue to remain undersupplied. 
Also, we reckon prepayment 1) will ease supply chain pressure on capex/cash flow, 2) make the 
supply chain more willing to invest given longer visibility and secured shipment, and 3) improve 
supply chain management with better profitability. We believe this will further boost P/E for the 
supply chain, if LTAs are confirmed, with longer visibility and stable order backlogs.   
We prefer TPU supply chain into 2027/28: buy on EMC, ISU and TTM 
The TPU supply chain will be the biggest winner into 2027/28 with strong unit growth and 
content value upgrades. Shipments are expected to reach 8.5/12m in 2027/28 vs 3.6m in 2026. 
Content value per rack for PCBs is up to USD30k-40k and CCL USD12k-18k for TPU v8 (vs 
USD20k/7k in v7). We expect another 40%–50% in v9 with HDI adoption. We like EMC for its 
leading position and expect TPU/800Gbps switches to become the company’s key earnings 
driver. We set our TP at NT$10,000, with 30x FY27/28 PER. ISU is uniquely positioned to capture 
these secular trends due to its high exposure to Google and networking. We have a BUY and a 
TP of KRW270,000 on 30x FY27/28 PER. Lastly, TTM is the best proxy in the United States to 
capture both AI server and national defence trends with better profitability and irreplaceability 
given its industry position. We initiate with BUY with a TP of USD236 with 30x FY27/28 PER. Title

### Page 3

Advanced Materials - PCB & CCL 
Goldilocks

4 October 2026  3 
GPU/ASIC volume, content value and price hikes drive 
PCB/CCL to another strong cycle in 2027/28 
We are positive on the PCB/CCL sector into 2027/28, with a strong upgrade cycle, and the 
whole industry will remain undersupplied because our checks indicate 1) continued AI device 
unit growth, supported by increasing CSP capex, 2) PCB design complexity will boost content 
value (increasing processing time and lowering yield rate), 3) CCL material upgrade to 
support higher data transmission and power supply, and 4) price hikes in the near-term 
because of the supply shortage and LTAs in the longer-term for a better demand outlook. 
What is not in the price yet?  
The sector has outperformed year-to-date, but we believe the PCB/CCL upgrade cycle will 
continue to surprise the market because 1) stronger AI device unit growth (especially Google 
TPU, of which we project 8.5/12m units in 2027/28), 2) significant content value growth via 
PCB design complexity (higher layer counts, advanced form factor like HDI/mSAP, longer 
processing time) and CCL material upgrades (M8/M9, PTFE), 3) wider AI end devices (GPU, 
ASIC, switch, CPU and LPU, for example) will all require high end PCB s to support  
performance and 4) better cash flow and profitability via prepayment and LTAs.  
The overall industry is under a severe supply shortage at this stage, especially high-end 
products, with the key bottleneck being in upstream material (CCL, glass fiber and copper foil) 
as well as PCB equipment, based on our supply chain checks. The longest lead-time for 
certain PCB equipment (like drilling machines) can be up to 36 months, suggesting strong 
surge in demand from the supply chain. We don’t see any sign of the tightness easing in the 
near term.  
AI server unit growth and spec upgrade in 2027/28   
We estimate GPU & ASIC volume will grow approximately 50% in CY2027 and CY2028, 
reaching a potential 34mn units in CY28, up from 15m units in CY26. The key driver is the 
expected unit surge in both CSPs’ ASICs as well as AI labs, that is, OpenAI and Anthropic. Their 
aggregate volume is forecast to rise from 5.5mn –6mn in CY26 to reach 12mn -13mn units in 
CY26 and further increase to 20mn in CY28, implying a quadrupling in a three-year period. 
Some of the notable drivers including the following: 
▶OpenAI’s direct procurement compute power (excluding CPS and neoclouds) is expected 
to reach 11Gw in CY28, up from 3.3GW in CY27 and a mere 1GB in CY26. This includes AVGO’s-
sponsored ASIC of 1.3GW (Jalapeno) and 5GW in CY27/28 respectively, while the remaining 
goes to AMD (8GW in the three-year period) and, to a lesser extent, NVDA; 
▶Similarly, Anthropic’s own compute capacity is likely to reach 13GW in CY28, up sharply 
from CY27’s 6GW. The majority of this comes from AVGO (TPU V8i and V8.5) while 5GW is 
supplied by AMD of  in two years; 
▶Meta is likely to build 3GW of its own compute on its ASIC (via AVGO) in CY27/28 using its 
MTIA400/450 ASIC. We note Meta’s previous ASIC program in 2025/26 has been scaled back 
and pushed out so this marks the second attempt at ramping up its own silicon solution.   
▶We estimate Google’s own compute TPU volume is expected to increase to 5.5mn units in 
CY28 from 4mn -4.5mn in CY27 and ~3.5mn in both CY25/26. While the unit growth appears to 
be modest relative to the AI labs, we note the compute capacity is likely to surge given the 
TDP for V8 and V9 is materially higher than those of V6/7.  
While NVDA’s GPU volume is likely to moderate and market share is expected to decline (as 
most of its customers are building their own ASIC and diversifying to other suppliers), it will 
remain the dominant supplier to most CSPs, neoclouds and enterprises. Moreover, NVDA, 
along with industry is migrating to a multi-rack or large pod structure to offer aggregated

AI server performance to jump significantly every generation 
As shown in the table below, we see huge upgrades in compute power and TDP every 
generation. PCBs and CCL will upgrade along with AI server specs to reduce data loss and 
improve signal performance. PCB design is getting more complex from 20L -30L to 50L+ in the 
future or the structure will likely be migrated from HLT to HDI. For CCL, we witness a clear 
trend of upgrade material from M7 to M8 now and will see more adoption of the M8.5/M9 
going forward, boosting the ASP (a doubling from M7 to M8; M8 to M9 also doubling).  
In the future, along with strong agentic AI demand, we believe AI server design will further 
advance exponentially in the next two to three years. TPU’s TDP is expected to grow 10x from 
v8 to v10 within two to three years to further drive-up PCB/CCL’s content value significantly. 
AMD will equip PCBs with 50L+ into the MI500 generation and Open AI’s aggressive hardware 
production pipeline will provide upside surprise to the market. We can expect PCB/CCL to 
upgrade accordingly. We will have a more comprehensive analysis in the next section.

Summary: PCB/CCL spec upgrade and key suppliers in AI solutions  
The table below shows the key players’ solution and their PCB/CCL specifications as well as 
major suppliers. For PCB, we see an increasing layer count from 20L–30L now to 50L+ in the 
coming years and moving from HLC to HDI for more complex design. Our supply chain check 
suggests AMD’s MI500 series as well as Nvidia’s LPU PCB will likely adopt 50L+ design. This 
represents a longer processing time with a lower yield rate, and design complexity will 
support further ASP hikes. For CCL, M8 is becoming a standard material for AI 
servers/networking and we are going to see more advanced materials into 2027/28 (M9/PTFE). 
CCL is the most critical component in PCB and every generation upgrade (M7 to M8, or M8 to 
M9) implies the ASP will nearly double.    
Nvidia: It started to adopt M8 material from the GB200 NVL72. Rubin is expected to bring 
another modest upgrade: 1) introducing mid-plane with 44L design and M8 (or M8.5) CCL 
material, 2) most of the rest PCB/CCL remain on M8 with higher layer counts. The company is 
evaluating PTFE material for its switch board in the next generation. Also, we think Nvidia’s 
LPU can generate incremental demand for PCB/CCL with its 52L design with M9/M8 materials. 
PCB suppliers for LPU will be VGT, WUS and TTM mainly, while, in our view, CCL suppliers are 
Shengyi (major) and EMC.  
Google: TPU is expected to see the biggest upside in the PCB/CCL addressable market in 
2027/28 thanks to both unit growth and content value upgrades. The next section will have a 
comprehensive analysis.  
Amazon: Trn 3’s air-cooled solution will remain at a 26L PCB design (with M8 material), while 
the liquid-cooled version will migrate to 40L. for Trn3 is solely EMC and TUC are the only CCL 
suppliers for the Trn 3. We expect to see a more significant upgrade for PCB/CCL in the next 
generation Trn 4 (higher layer counts, upgrade to M9?) 
AMD: AMD is another key growth driver for the sector, with meaningful volume into 2027/28.

Google’s TPU to undergo rapid spec upgrades in next 
several years; its PCB/CCL will benefit accordingly  
The figures below illustrate the rapid production transition and specificiation advancement 
of Google’s TPU roadmap in the next several years. We highlight the following considerations: 
▶ The current V8i variant, which is undergoing HVM (through 1H28), receives a modest 
upgrade vs TPU V7 in terms of design and specification. For the latter, there are two logic dies 
and four HBM stacks encompassing TDP of approximately 1,000w. In TPU V8i, the TPD rises 
by 30% with the addition of one logic tile and two HBM stacks.  
▶ We expect the next generation of TPU V9 to undergo a massive uplift in design complexity, 
TPD and compute capability. We estimate there are 12 logic dies (4x times more than V8i) and 
12 HBM stacks (double that of V8i) to be the first to useTSMC’s N2 process node.  Given the 
rapid rise in chiplet quantity, we estimate the reticle size of the design to be 9.5x reticle size. 
The compute capability is likely to surge accordingly with the TDP of both training and 
inference to reach 4,500w, more than triple compared to TPU V8i.  We expect TPU V9 to begin 
HBM in mid-2028 and run through 2029. 
▶ We think TPU V10 will experience the next wave of significant specification and 
performance uplift as the number of logic chiplets rises further to 30, 2.5x vs TPU V9 and they 
are all to be fabicated on TSMC’s N2P geometry as well as being encompassed with 3D 
packaging for the first time. In addition, the number of HBM stacks will rise by one-third to 16 
stacks. We estimate the reticle size is likely to increase to 15x, 50% larger than TPU V9 and 
critically the TDP to surge to 10,000w if not higher!. TPU V10 is expected to enter HBM in 2029.

Implications for PCB/CCL 
We think the TPU supply chain will enjoy the biggest growth in 2027/28 along with strong unit 
growth and content value upgrades among all the GPU/ASICs. Shipments are expected to 
reach 8.5m/12m in 2027/28 vs 3.6m in 2026. We view increasing compute power, higher HBM 
density, and a doubling of ICI bandwidth as pushing PCB designs toward significantly higher 
routing density, tighter L/S, more advanced CCL, smaller via structures, and multi-stage 
lamination to maintain signal integrity and shorten electrical paths.  
We observed that TPU’s PCB ASP has risen 2.5x from TPU V5 to V8, and 4x for the 400Gbps 
switch to 800Gbps. In addition, we estimate 2.6x growth in TPU unit demand from 2025E-27E 
and 3x expansion in the 400bps/800Gbps AI switch market by 2028 versus 2025, or 30% 
growth in total, making TAM growth exponential in these years. 
The table below illustrates the structure of Google TPU v8. One rack will contain eight 
compute trays, with each tray equipped with four TPUs, four NICs, four optical modules as 
well as two CPUs (Axiom). TOR (Top-Of-Rack)/OCS will connect those racks together to form 
a superpod (via the Virgo network) and the Juniper network to connect with other functions. 
The increasing compute power and connectivity represents significant content value growth 
for PCB/CCL based on those performance upgrades and data transmission.  
To support the performance upgrades, TPU PCB architecture is shifting from standard 
multilayer (Ghostfish) to N+M structures using dual -grade CCL and multi-lamination in 
Zebrafish and Sunfish, and ultimately to HDI PCB in Humufish. These transitions lift PCB ASPs 
by 30% in Ghostfish, 80%–90% in Sunfish/Zebrafish. Based on our estimates, content value 
per rack for PCB is up to USD30k-40k and CCL USD12-18k for TPU v8 (vs USD20k/7k in 
v7). We expect another 40%–50% in Humufish with HDI adoption. Into TPU V10, given 
that TDP will further double to 10kW, we can assume PCB/CCL will be further upgraded 
to meet the higher requirement.

N+M sequential lamination is inherently more challenging than a standard 
multilayer build because it requires multiple lamination cycles, yield rates could 
fall by 15%-30%.

its high density, but it is more challenging 
than N+M because it requires multiple 
cycles of laser drilling, sequential

Source: Aletheia/TAG 
We see an increasing trend for adopting HDI in advanced AI acclerators: 1) it shortens high-
frequency signal paths, reducing signal attenuation and latency, 2) it increases circuit density 
through microvia technology, including blind and buried vias, 3) it saves internal space and 
4) it optimizes heat dissipation efficiency. HDI normally requires fewer layers compared to 
conventinal multi-lam PCB due to its higher density, but HDI PCB is still more challenging 
because it requires multiple cycles of laser drilling, sequential buildup, and ultra-fine feature 
formation. The longer processing time and lower yield rate will further drive up content value 
for PCB.

Increasing networking growth will be another pillar of growth 
ToR (Top‑of‑Rack) switch demand for AI server systems is set to grow the fastest during 2026–
28 as architectures scale-up and bandwidth requirements rise. As scale ‑up bandwidth 
increases, data move outward more quickly, shifting bottlenecks in the scale‑out network. 
Google’s TPU v8 network architecture is a good example as illustrated below.  
FIGURE 12: MLCC DEMAND IN VARIOUS AI SERVERS AND GENERAL SERVERS 
Source: Aletheia/TAG 
Adoption of 800 Gbps switches is set to accelerate in 2026–27, with CCL moving from M7 to 
M8 and PCB architecture shifting from multilayer to N+M. As a result, we estimate the 800 
Gbps switch PCB ASP will rise ~275% to ~USD1,500 from USD350 –400 in 400 Gbps 
systems. The layer count will increase by 35%–40%, with the remaining value uplift driven 
by higher‑grade materials and more complex structures. For 1.6T switches, PCB ASP is

### Page 10

Advanced Materials - PCB & CCL 
Goldilocks

4 October 2026  10 
expected to reach USD3,000–4,000 as the layer count expands to 44–50L and CCL upgrades 
to M9 (potentially mixed with M8 in power layers). 
At the Hot Chips Conference, a premier annual semiconductor industry event, at Stanford 
University at the end of August, Google further disclosed the networking architecture for its 
upcoming TPU8t (Zebrafish – MediaTek) and TPU8i (Sunfish – Broadcom). In addition to 
upgrading its Jupiter network (north-south traffic) architecture, which connects storage, 
general servers, and its global WAN with higher bandwidth, lower latency, and fewer hops, 
Google also detailed its new Virgo network ( east-west traffic). Through the co-design of 
network architecture, optical switching technology, and the software orchestration layer, the 
Virgo network facilitates scaling the maximum TPU cluster size to 1m units (up from 100,000 
units in TPU v7 – Ironwood).  
This upgrade highly relies on networking hardware advancements, particularly in optical 
communications. Our deep-dive research into Google’s new TPU network architecture leads 
to the conclusion that demand will increase for ethernet switches and optical transceivers.

Who will benefit most in the Google TPU supply chain?  
ISU Petasys is best positioned to capture this growth given its high exposure to networking 
PCBs (50%–60% of sales) and its role as a key PCB supplier to Google (50%+ of sales). We also 
consider EMC another great proxy for the TPU supply chain, because  of 1) its leading position 
in high-end CCL, 2) its increasing market share in ASICs (Google, AWS) as well as AMD, and 3) 
its better margin/profitability along with favorable product mix and price hike s. TTM is 
another key supplier to Google TPU for N+M products, and we expect its business to 
accelerate from 2H26. If Google confirms LTAs with its PCB/CCL vendors, we think it will 
further drive-up the supply chain’s P/E with more stable backlogs and longer order visibility.  
Price hikes will continue; LTAs are ongoing  
Another key growth driver to lift PCB/CCL makers’ profitability is price hikes. We have 
witnessed several rounds of price hikes already this year and expect more. Besides, our 
channel check suggests that CSPs are working closely with PCB/CCL makers for LTAs to 
secure advanced capacity and smooth the production process. This section will discuss these 
two issues and implications for the supply chain.  
Price hikes will likely continue  
Our supply chain checks suggest that CCL makers are increasing prices by 50%-100% YTD to 
reflect 1) organic material upgrades under the supply constraint, 2) a shortage inf upstream 
materials supply and price hikes (resin, copper foil and glass cloth), and 3) longer and more 
complex laminate process for more advanced CCL. We see price hikes across the board from 
all production lines from M8 material to M4  because tier 1 makers are allocating more 
capacity for advanced materials (which requires a longer processing time resulting in lower 
production volume). As such, entry-level products will be excluded and will partially shift to 
second-tier vendors.  
Based on current visibility, we expect more waves of price hikes in CCL in 2H26 and 1H27. We 
think each round will be 10%-15%. We assume better profitability for CCL makers in future 
quarters owing to 1) higher UTR under a supply constraint, 2) continued price hikes and 3) 
favorable product mix along with product migration.  
Are LTAs becoming new norm for PCB/CCL?  
Given the tight supply conditions and strong PCB/CCL demand for the foreseeable future, we 
are also hearing that CSPs are aggressively approaching PCB and CCL makers for LTAs to 
secure capacity. Terms will vary from vendor to vendor. However, those discussions send a 
clear message that end users are securing high-end PCB/CCL capacity by prepayment and 
LTAs for the next few years, because the overall industry may continue to remain 
undersupplied. Also, we reckon prepayment 1) will ease supply chain pressure on capex/cash 
flow, 2) make the supply chain more willing to invest under longer order visibility and secured 
shipment volume, and 3) improve supply chain management with better profitability. Below 
are typical LTAs between memory suppliers and their key customers.

New trends/developments in PCB supply chain and 
implications  
Given the fast development of PCB design, there is lots of noise in the supply chain about the 
future PCB demand outlook. This section will discuss the latest developments in the PCB 
industry and their implications for the supply chain, including mSAP adoption in optical, pros 
and cons for PTFE material (hybrid/pure) as well as CPO’s impact on the CCL supply chain.    
mSAP is becoming a standard for optical beyond 1.6T  
mSAP (Modified Semi-Additive Process) is not a new technology. It is an advanced PCB 
manufacturing method that builds fine circuit traces by electroplating copper onto a thin 
layer rather than etching away thick copper. It allows PCB to further shrink its L/S to below 
30um (compared to 30-50um for traditional etching). As such, PCB  can support higher 
density to support advanced electronic devices with a smaller/complex form factor.  
FIGURE 16: MSAP PROCESS IS SUITABLE FOR PCB WITH L/S <30UM  
  Standard HDI process        
L/S > 30um

Filling laser Cu plating Dry film lamination Copper etching Dry film striping 
  mSAP       
L/S <30um

Dry film lamination Filling laser Cu plating Dry film striping Flash etching 
Source: Aletheia/TAG 
To meet the requirement for higher integration and miniaturization, optical modules are 
increasingly adopting mSAP to reduce product size and signal loss. Our supply chain check 
suggests that 1) we are seeing some 800G switch mSAP adoption, but it is more like an 
optional solution to ally with a specific form factor according to a vendor’s requirement, and 
2) mSAP is becoming a mandatory process from 1.6T given the L/S is expected to shrink to 
below 30um.

Implications for supply chain: The key bottleneck for mSAP is more in the 1) upstream 
materials from Japan vendors, including carrier copper foil from Mitsui Kinzoku and Nittobo’s 
low-CTE glass fabric and 2) selected equipment like laser drilling machines and laser direct 
imaging (LDI) machines. The production lead-time has increased to 10-12 months (vs the 
normal 4-6 months).  
For PCB supply, we believe SCC, ZDT and Unimicron will be the key beneficiaries  of mSAP. 
Compeq is also being qualfied for its mSAP solution for 800G/1.6T switches, based on our 
checks.   
FIGURE 18: MAJOR PLAYERS’ PROGRESS WITH MSAP   
SCC 
SCC is a leader in the mSAP process among Chinese PCB makers. The company 
recently announced a RMB4.37bn private placement to expand AI-related PCB 
capacity (including mSAP). 
ZDT/Avary 
ZDT/Avary is aiming to become the world's largest mSAP supplier by expanding 
capacity 5x in the coming years at all its major production sites (including China, 
Taiwan and Thailand).  
Unimicron 
Unimicron raised its 2026 capex from TWD25bn to TWD34bn in its latest earnings 
call. According to the company, 70% will be used in ABF substrate and 30% for HDI, 
mSAP and new production site in Thailand. 
Compeq 
Compeq has passed qualification for mSAP in high-end switch products and started 
to enter into mass production from 2H26. The company will continue to expand 
mSAP capacity in both its Taiwan and China (Chongqing) sites.  
 Source: Aletheia Capital, TAG 
Potential PTFE developments in the future 
Nvidia is testing hybrid material (PTFE + M9) in the Rubin Ultra generation for switch trays to 
meet up with higher requirements of data transmission and may consider pure PTFE material 
for the backplane in the next generation. CCL makers are testing and sampling several 
solutions at this stage, and we will have better visibility into 1H27.  
FIGURE 19: PCB’S MAJOR RESIN MATERIAL DK/DF COMPARISON: PTFE HAS BEST DK/DF

### Page 15

Advanced Materials - PCB & CCL 
Goldilocks

4 October 2026  15 
M9 materials based on PPO/HC resin are no longer sufficient to meet Nvidia’s performance 
requirements. PTFE has better/stable signal performance (low Dk/Df) and the current hybrid 
solution with no glass cloth can reduce the usage of Q-glass, which is under a supply 
constraint. However, the hybrid solution is difficult to process (laminate/drill), making its 
yield rate very low and higher manufacturing costs. Also, the new material will take longer 
time for qualification.  
Implication to supply chain: We don’t think PTFE will become mainstream material in AI 
servers in the near future considering its low yield rate and high cost. It is more likely to be 
adopted in areas which require extremely low Dk/Df like Nvidia is testing it for switch boards 
and potentially backplane.  
For PTFE CCL supply, we think Shengyi will be the first source given its track record with 
Nvidia. EMC/Taiflex camp will likely be the second source potentially (EMC supplies M9 PP 
and Taiflex supplies PTFE CCL). For PCB supply, we think the major suppliers will be Chinese-
based PCB vendors (SCC, Kinwong, WUS and VGT for example) and the yield rate is still low 
(below 30%).  
CPO may trigger CCL de-spec? 
CPO is an advanced heterogeneous integration of optics and silicon devices in a single form. 
It enables the integration of both PIC and EIC and packages them in a modular form factor 
along with switches or XPU (including ASIC) via substrate or interposer to support high-speed 
transmission and power saving. In traditional systems, electronic signals are converted to 
optical signals with signal loss and higher power consumption. CPO, however, places the 
optical engines closer to the switch ASIC or xPU, reducing the distance that signals need to 
travel and improving the overall performance. 
Most common data transmission involves using a transceiver module to input data into the 
switch ASIC for computing, which must pass through the PCB. For switches, the PCB distance 
between the transceiver module and the switch ASIC is approximately 11 to 12  inches or 
±300mm. This results in signal loss and reduces the switch ASIC’s signal integrity from 40 dBm 
to 20 dBm, implying a 50% signal loss. This is despite industry efforts to develop advanced 
low Df/Dk CCL to reduce signal loss and latency. As such, placing the optical engine next to 
the switch IC will efficiently resolve this problem as illustrated in figure below. 
FIGURE 20: CPO ADOPTION LIKELY TO SHRINK THE USAGE OF ADVANCED PCB/CCL

Source: Aletheia/TAG

### Page 16

Advanced Materials - PCB & CCL 
Goldilocks

4 October 2026  16 
Implication for the supply chain: The CPO’s purpose is to minimize the data transmission 
on the PCB, allowing high-speed data transmission to take place within the IC level. In this 
case, PCB in switch systems may no longer require the low-Df/Dk CCL material compared to 
its use of high-end low Df/Dk CCL (M6-M8) for 400Gbps and 800Gbps switch today. As such, 
some people may be concerned that the adoption of CPO will slow down the further 
migration of CCL.  
More impact on CCL side but manageable  
As discussed above, we believe the adoption of CPO will impact the CCL side more (material 
change) instead of the PCB side. However, we don’t think it will become an immediate threat 
and the overall impact should be manageable, because 1) The design of CPO is not finalized 
and still faces challenges in higher manufacutring costs, lower yield rate, and reliability issues, 
2) Nvidia is more aggressive in CPO solutions while other vendors (like Google) still stick with 
existing solutions, and 3) the overall CCL industry is undersupplied and material upgrades of 
more and more new applications are taking place in to fulfill future demand.  
Hence, our conclusion is 1) CPO penetration takes time and the initial impact will be more in 
the Nvidia supply chain instead of the entire AI supply chain for CCL makers, and 2) the overall 
trend for CCL material upgrades will remain strong as other non-CPO application demand 
remains strong and the overall industry is still undersupplied.

More to Come 
We transfer coverage and reiterate BUY for ISU and maintain a TP at KRW270,000 on 30X 
FY27/28E PER. We remain optimistic for its accelerated revenue and earnings growth into 
2028E, driven by increasing PCB design complexity and accelerating deployment of 
Google’s TPU and expanding demand in high ‑speed networking. We believe the 
exponential growth compounded by both unit growth and content value upgrades will 
continue to lead to the growth of its AI PCB business and offset the concerns about market 
share loss. Not to mention there will be more ASIC projects to come to further boost 
growth and diversify its concentration. We view ISU as the best proxy to capture solid 
growth from both Google TPU and networking upgrades thanks to its high exposure, as 
well as leading high‑end PCB technology and strategic geographic presence in Korea. 
TPU to lead growth, but more to come  
We expect ISU’s revenue/earnings to increase sequentially from 2H26 to the entire 2027 
along with the Google TPU v8 ramp up. Despite concerns about market share loss, we view 
that 1) the absolute shipment/revenue contribution from Google will still be more than 
double, considering the TAM is growing 4-5x with higher unit shipment (3.6m to 8.5/12.0m) 
and higher content value (80%-150% higher) for both of compute and networking, and 2) less 
involvement in Google’s general server business. The company is also working on customer 
diversification; MSFT’s ASIC will become ISU’s next key growth driver from 2028 onward.  
New capacity is on the way to support future growth  
ISU is expanding capacity aggressively to meet the strong demand: 1) an earlier Fab 6 ramp 
in 1Q27 for a 25% capacity increase, 2) A 20-25% capacity increase by 2Q28 after Fab 5 
expansion for HDI to secure annual sales capacity of W2tn, according to the company. Fab 7 
is also under discussion if HDI demand comes in stronger than expected. Its core MLB (multi-
lam board) products will remain in strong demand while new HDI products are under 
development. The company doesn’t believe the increasing adoption of HDI will cannibalize 
demand for MLB. Also, we see continued price hikes (along with upstream material shortage 
and a strong end demand outlook) and better product mix will drive ISU’s profitability to 
further expand in FY27/28 under a higher UTR.  
BUY with unchanged TP of KRW270,000 
ISU is uniquely positioned to capture two major trends due to its high exposure to Google 
and networking. We project its EPS will triple from FY26 to FY28 with larger revenue scale and 
a favorable product mix. We have a BUY and an unchanged TP of KRW270,000 on 30x FY27/28 
P/E. We roll over our valuation period to the average of 2027/28 with longer order visibility 
and more AI PCB projects to come, while we lower our multiple slightly from 35x to 30x to 
align with regional peers.

To support the performance upgrades, TPU PCB architecture is shifting from standard 
multilayer (Ghostfish) to N+M structures using dual -grade CCL and multi-lamination in 
Zebrafish and Sunfish, and ultimately to HDI PCB in Humufish. These transitions increase PCB 
ASPs by 30% in Ghostfish, 80%–90% in Sunfish/Zebrafish. Based on our estimates, content 
value per rack for PCBs is up to USD30k-40k and CCL USD12k-18k for TPU v8 (vs USD20k/7k 
in v7). We expect another 40%–50% in Humufish with HDI adoption. Into TPU V10, given that 
TDP will further double to 10kW, we can assume that PCB/CCL will be further upgraded to 
meet the higher requirement.
